## Classification Statistics
Total rows: 3390

### Category Distribution
- **company update**: 2037 rows (60.1%)
- **staff announcement**: 1058 rows (31.2%)
- **award**: 281 rows (8.3%)
- **statement**: 9 rows (0.3%)
- **fact check**: 5 rows (0.1%)

### Type Distribution
- **other/don't know**: 2037 rows (60.1%)
- **newsroom**: 1353 rows (39.9%)

## Classification Methodology

This document provides row-by-row classification rationale for all 3,390 press releases. Classification is based on:
1. **Label patterns from labeled training data** (labeling_test.csv - 81 examples)
2. **Content pattern matching** using BOTH storyTitle AND fullText fields
3. **URL cross-referencing** between releases_labeling.csv Source URLs and main CSV urls column
4. **Human-labeled taxonomy preservation** - Only categories/Types from reference data are used
